#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

// Types to represent unstructured mesh elements
using idx_t = uint64_t;
using val_t = double;

// Maximum number of connections per element (for a 2D grid: 4 neighbors)
constexpr int MAX_CONNECTIONS = 8;

// Material properties for energy transfer
struct Material {
    val_t transfer_coeff;  // Energy transfer coefficient
    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink
};

// Static connectivity information for each element
struct ElementStatic {
    idx_t material_idx;
    idx_t num_connections;
    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements
    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection
};

// Dynamic state for each element
struct ElementDynamic {
    val_t current_energy;
    val_t total_flux;
};

// World state
struct World {
    std::vector<Material> materials;
    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;
    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;
};

// Material type IDs
constexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;
constexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;
constexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;

// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh
// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries
void buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {
    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
    
    // Initialize materials
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material
    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material
    
    // Allocate elements
    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);
    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);
    
    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy
    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;
        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;
        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;
    }
    
    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid
    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {
        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {
            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;
            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];
            
            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)
            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};
            
            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {
                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];
                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];
                
                // Check if neighbor is within bounds
                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {
                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;
                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;
                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;
                    elem.num_connections++;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics
    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;
    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;
}

// Compute energy flux between two elements
inline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,
                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {
    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * 
           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;
}

// Run simulation for n_iters iterations
void runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {
    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {
        // Update all elements
        for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {
            const ElementStatic& elem_static = world.elements_static[i];
            const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[i];
            const Material& mat = world.materials[elem_static.material_idx];
            
            // Start with external flow
            val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;
            
            // Add flux from all connected elements
            for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {
                const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];
                const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = world.elements_dynamic[neighbor_idx];
                total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);
            }
            
            // Update element state
            ElementDynamic& elem_write = world.elements_dynamic_swap[i];
            elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;
            elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);
        }
        
        // Swap buffers
        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);
    }
}

// Validate simulation results
bool validateResults(const World& world) {
    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    
    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;
        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;
        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);
        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);
    }
    
    printf("Validation results:\n");
    printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
    printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
    printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
    
    // Check for numerical issues
    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {
        printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
    return true;
}

// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification
uint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {
    uint64_t hash = 0;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {
        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values
        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);
        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);
        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    }
    return hash;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    int n_elems_root = 512;
    int n_iters = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;
    
    printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
    printf("============================================\n");
    printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\n", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", n_iters);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    printf("\n");
    
    // Build the unstructured mesh
    printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
    World world;
    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);
    
    // Calculate memory usage
    const size_t static_mem = world.elements_static.size() * sizeof(ElementStatic);
    const size_t dynamic_mem = world.elements_dynamic.size() * sizeof(ElementDynamic) * 2;
    const size_t total_mem = static_mem + dynamic_mem;
    printf("Memory usage: %.2f MB (static: %.2f MB, dynamic: %.2f MB)\n",
           total_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
           static_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
           dynamic_mem / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
    printf("\n");
    
    // Run simulation
    printf("Running simulation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    runSimulation(world, n_iters);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration_ms = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start).count();
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration_ms);
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    const int n_measured_iters = std::max(n_iters - 1, 1);
    const double time_per_iter = static_cast<double>(duration_ms) / n_measured_iters;
    const double giga_elems_per_sec = (n_measured_iters * n_elems) / (duration_ms / 1000.0) / 1e9;
    
    // Approximate FLOPS: ~22 FLOPS per element per iteration (from reference)
    const double gflops = giga_elems_per_sec * 22.0;
    
    printf("Performance:\n");
    printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iter);
    printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n", giga_elems_per_sec);
    printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
    
    // Compute hash for verification
    const uint64_t hash = computeHash(world.elements_dynamic);
    printf("  Result hash: %016lX\n", hash);
    printf("\n");
    
    // Print results for external validation
    if (printResults) {
        std::vector<double> energyData;
        energyData.reserve(world.elements_dynamic.size());
        for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {
            energyData.push_back(elem.current_energy);
        }
        print_results(energyData, "ElementEnergy");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        bool valid = validateResults(world);
        if (!valid) {
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

codex
The kernel is a structured four-neighbor stencil represented through unstructured connectivity. I’m partitioning it by contiguous grid-row slabs: each rank stores only its owned elements plus two energy halo rows, exchanges those halos every iteration, and computes its local elements while communication is in flight. This keeps memory distributed and limits communication to nearest neighbors. Root-only reporting plus ordered collection/reductions will preserve the original CLI behavior.

#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

./output.txt:841:The kernel is a structured four-neighbor stencil represented through unstructured connectivity. I’m partitioning it by contiguous grid-row slabs: each rank stores only its owned elements plus two energy halo rows, exchanges those halos every iteration, and computes its local elements while communication is in flight. This keeps memory distributed and limits communication to nearest neighbors. Root-only reporting plus ordered collection/reductions will preserve the original CLI behavior.

+inline void updateElement(const Domain& domain, int local_x, int y,
+                          const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
+                          ElementDynamic* output) {
+    constexpr val_t transfer = 0.8 * 0.25;
+    const int n = domain.grid_size;
+    const int global_x = domain.first_row + local_x;
+    const size_t local_idx = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n + y;
+    const ElementDynamic& current = domain.elements[local_idx];
+

+                        (y == 0 || y == n - 1);
+    if (corner) {
+        // The two diagonal corners are inflows and the other two outflows.
+        total_flux = (global_x == y) ? 0.5 : -0.5;

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
+    uint64_t result = 0;

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {

+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,

+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<double> global;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
+        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
+        int offset = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < domain.ranks; ++rank) {
+            const int rows = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            counts[rank] = rows * domain.grid_size;
+            displacements[rank] = offset;
+            offset += counts[rank];
+        }
+        global.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.grid_size) * domain.grid_size);
     }
-    return hash;
+
+    // ElementDynamic consists of two doubles; send only energies using a
+    // vector datatype so no temporary packing buffer is needed on each rank.
+    MPI_Datatype energy_type;
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(domain.elements.size()), 1, 2, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    &energy_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&energy_type);
+    MPI_Gatherv(domain.elements.empty() ? nullptr
+                                       : &domain.elements[0].current_energy,
+                domain.elements.empty() ? 0 : 1, energy_type,
+                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&energy_type);
+    return global;
 }

+inline void updateElement(const Domain& domain, int local_x, int y,
+                          const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
+                          ElementDynamic* output) {
+    constexpr val_t transfer = 0.8 * 0.25;
+    const int n = domain.grid_size;
+    const int global_x = domain.first_row + local_x;
+    const size_t local_idx = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n + y;
+    const ElementDynamic& current = domain.elements[local_idx];
+
+    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
+    const bool corner = (global_x == 0 || global_x == n - 1) &&
+                        (y == 0 || y == n - 1);
+    if (corner) {
+        // The two diagonal corners are inflows and the other two outflows.
+        total_flux = (global_x == y) ? 0.5 : -0.5;
+    }

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
+    uint64_t result = 0;
+    const uint64_t global_begin =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.elements.size(); ++i) {
+        uint64_t energy_bits;

+                    sizeof(flux_bits));

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;

+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
+        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid) {
+            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
     }

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<double> global;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
+        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
+        int offset = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < domain.ranks; ++rank) {
+            const int rows = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            counts[rank] = rows * domain.grid_size;
+            displacements[rank] = offset;
+            offset += counts[rank];
+        }
+        global.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.grid_size) * domain.grid_size);
     }
-    return hash;
+
+    // ElementDynamic consists of two doubles; send only energies using a
+    // vector datatype so no temporary packing buffer is needed on each rank.
+    MPI_Datatype energy_type;
+    MPI_Type_vector(static_cast<int>(domain.elements.size()), 1, 2, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                    &energy_type);
+    MPI_Type_commit(&energy_type);
+    MPI_Gatherv(domain.elements.empty() ? nullptr
+                                       : &domain.elements[0].current_energy,
+                domain.elements.empty() ? 0 : 1, energy_type,
+                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Type_free(&energy_type);
+    return global;
 }

+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.elements.capacity() + domain.swap.capacity()) *

+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.upper_halo.capacity() +
+                              domain.lower_halo.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t);

+    uint64_t max_rank_memory = 0;

+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &max_rank_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Distributed memory usage: %.2f MB total, %.2f MB max/rank\n\n",
+                    total_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
+                    max_rank_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
+        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
+    }

+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    runSimulation(domain, iterations);
+    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
+        const double milliseconds = seconds * 1000.0;
+        const double time_per_iteration = milliseconds / measured_iterations;
+        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
+            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * element_count) /
+                  seconds / 1e9
+            : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance:\n");
+        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
+        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second);
+        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
+    }
+
+    if (printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
+        }
+    }
+
+    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return valid ? 0 : 1;

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {

#include <mpi.h>

#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <limits>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using val_t = double;

struct Domain {
    int grid_size = 0;
    int first_row = 0;
    int row_count = 0;
    int rank = 0;
    int ranks = 1;
    std::vector<val_t> energy;
    std::vector<val_t> total_flux;
    std::vector<val_t> energy_swap;
    std::vector<val_t> total_flux_swap;
    std::vector<val_t> upper_halo;
    std::vector<val_t> lower_halo;
};

// Use a balanced, contiguous row decomposition.  Contiguous rows make both
// halo messages contiguous and preserve global element ordering for output.
Domain buildSquare2D(int grid_size, int rank, int ranks) {
    Domain domain;
    domain.grid_size = grid_size;
    domain.rank = rank;
    domain.ranks = ranks;

    const int base = grid_size / ranks;
    const int remainder = grid_size % ranks;
    domain.row_count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    domain.first_row = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);

    const size_t local_elements =
        static_cast<size_t>(domain.row_count) * static_cast<size_t>(grid_size);
    domain.energy.resize(local_elements);
    domain.total_flux.resize(local_elements);
    domain.energy_swap.resize(local_elements);
    domain.total_flux_swap.resize(local_elements);
    if (domain.row_count != 0) {
        domain.upper_halo.resize(static_cast<size_t>(grid_size));
        domain.lower_halo.resize(static_cast<size_t>(grid_size));
    }
    return domain;
}

inline void updateElement(const Domain& domain, int local_x, int y,
                          const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
                          val_t* energy_output, val_t* flux_output) {
    constexpr val_t transfer = 0.8 * 0.25;
    const int n = domain.grid_size;
    const int global_x = domain.first_row + local_x;
    const size_t local_idx = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n + y;
    const val_t current = domain.energy[local_idx];

    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
    const bool corner = (global_x == 0 || global_x == n - 1) &&
                        (y == 0 || y == n - 1);
    if (corner) {
        // The two diagonal corners are inflows and the other two outflows.
        total_flux = (global_x == y) ? 0.5 : -0.5;
    }

    // Keep the original connection order: +x, -x, +y, -y.  This preserves
    // the floating-point result bit for bit, independent of the partition.
    if (global_x + 1 < n) {
        const val_t neighbor = (local_x + 1 < domain.row_count)
            ? domain.energy[local_idx + n]
            : lower[y];
        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * transfer;
    }
    if (global_x > 0) {
        const val_t neighbor = (local_x > 0)
            ? domain.energy[local_idx - n]
            : upper[y];
        total_flux += (neighbor - current) * transfer;
    }
    if (y + 1 < n) {
        total_flux += (domain.energy[local_idx + 1] - current) * transfer;
    }
    if (y > 0) {
        total_flux += (domain.energy[local_idx - 1] - current) * transfer;
    }

    energy_output[local_idx] = current + total_flux;
    flux_output[local_idx] = domain.total_flux[local_idx] + std::abs(total_flux);
}

void updateRows(const Domain& domain, int begin, int end,
                const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
                val_t* energy_output, val_t* flux_output) {
    for (int local_x = begin; local_x < end; ++local_x) {
        for (int y = 0; y < domain.grid_size; ++y) {
            updateElement(domain, local_x, y, upper, lower, energy_output,
                          flux_output);
        }
    }
}

void runSimulation(Domain& domain, int iterations) {
    if (domain.row_count == 0) {
        return;
    }

    const int previous = domain.first_row == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : domain.rank - 1;
    const int next = domain.first_row + domain.row_count == domain.grid_size
        ? MPI_PROC_NULL : domain.rank + 1;
    const int n = domain.grid_size;

    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        MPI_Request requests[4];
        int request_count = 0;

        if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            MPI_Irecv(domain.upper_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101,
                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE,
                      previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                      &requests[request_count++]);
        }
        if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
            MPI_Irecv(domain.lower_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102,
                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
            const size_t last_row = static_cast<size_t>(domain.row_count - 1) * n;
            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data() + last_row, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
                      next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
                      &requests[request_count++]);
        }

        // Rows not touching a rank boundary do not depend on incoming halos.
        if (domain.row_count > 2) {
            updateRows(domain, 1, domain.row_count - 1,
                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
        }
        if (request_count != 0) {
            MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
        }

        updateRows(domain, 0, 1, domain.upper_halo.data(),
                   domain.lower_halo.data(), domain.swap.data());
                   domain.lower_halo.data(), domain.energy_swap.data(),
                   domain.total_flux_swap.data());
        if (domain.row_count > 1) {
            updateRows(domain, domain.row_count - 1, domain.row_count,
                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
        }
        domain.energy.swap(domain.energy_swap);
        domain.total_flux.swap(domain.total_flux_swap);
    }
}

uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
    uint64_t result = 0;
    const uint64_t global_begin =
        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;
    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
        uint64_t energy_bits;
        uint64_t flux_bits;
        std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &domain.energy[i],
                    sizeof(energy_bits));
        std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &domain.total_flux[i],
                    sizeof(flux_bits));
        const uint64_t global_i = global_begin + i;
        result ^= (energy_bits + global_i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
        result ^= (flux_bits + global_i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
    }
    return result;
}

bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t local_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
        local_energy_sum += domain.energy[i];
        local_flux_sum += domain.total_flux[i];
        local_max = std::max(local_max, domain.energy[i]);
        local_min = std::min(local_min, domain.energy[i]);
    }

    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    int valid = 1;
    if (domain.rank == 0) {
        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
            valid = 0;
        }
        if (valid) {
            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
        }
    }
    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    return valid != 0;
}

std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
    std::vector<int> counts;
    std::vector<int> displacements;
    std::vector<double> global;
    if (domain.rank == 0) {
        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
        int offset = 0;
        for (int rank = 0; rank < domain.ranks; ++rank) {
            const int rows = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
            counts[rank] = rows * domain.grid_size;
            displacements[rank] = offset;
            offset += counts[rank];
        }
        global.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.grid_size) * domain.grid_size);
    }

    MPI_Gatherv(domain.energy.data(), static_cast<int>(domain.energy.size()),
                MPI_DOUBLE,
                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    return global;
}

void printUsage(const char* program) {
    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
    std::printf("Options:\n");
    std::printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
    int rank = 0;
    int ranks = 1;
    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);

    int grid_size = 512;
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    int parse_status = 0;
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            grid_size = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            parse_status = 2;
        } else {
            if (rank == 0) {
                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            }
            parse_status = 1;
        }
    }
    if (grid_size <= 0 || iterations < 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            std::printf("Grid size must be positive and iterations non-negative.\n");
        }
        parse_status = 1;
    }
    if (parse_status != 0) {
        if (rank == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
        }
        MPI_Finalize();
        return parse_status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
    }

    const uint64_t element_count =
        static_cast<uint64_t>(grid_size) * static_cast<uint64_t>(grid_size);
    if (rank == 0) {
        std::printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
        std::printf("============================================\n");
        std::printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %llu elements\n", grid_size,
                    grid_size, static_cast<unsigned long long>(element_count));
        std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", ranks);
        std::printf("Validation: %s\n\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
        std::printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
    }

    Domain domain = buildSquare2D(grid_size, rank, ranks);
    const uint64_t local_memory =
        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.energy.capacity() +
                              domain.total_flux.capacity() +
                              domain.energy_swap.capacity() +
                              domain.total_flux_swap.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t) +
        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.upper_halo.capacity() +
                              domain.lower_halo.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t);
    uint64_t total_memory = 0;
    uint64_t max_rank_memory = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &total_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &max_rank_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    if (rank == 0) {
        std::printf("Distributed memory usage: %.2f MB total, %.2f MB max/rank\n\n",
                    total_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
                    max_rank_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
    }

    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
    runSimulation(domain, iterations);
    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
    double seconds = 0.0;
    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(domain);
    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0,
               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

    if (rank == 0) {
        const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
        const double milliseconds = seconds * 1000.0;
        const double time_per_iteration = milliseconds / measured_iterations;
        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * element_count) /
                  seconds / 1e9
            : 0.0;
        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
        std::printf("Performance:\n");
        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
                    giga_elements_per_second);
        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
    }

    if (printResults) {
    if (printResults) {
        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
        if (rank == 0) {
            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
        }
    }

    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
    MPI_Finalize();
    return valid ? 0 : 1;
}

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

+            MPI_Irecv(domain.upper_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
+            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                      &requests[request_count++]);
+        }
+        if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
+            MPI_Irecv(domain.lower_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102,
+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
+            const size_t last_row = static_cast<size_t>(domain.row_count - 1) * n;
+            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data() + last_row, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
+                      next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                      &requests[request_count++]);
+        }
+
+        // Rows not touching a rank boundary do not depend on incoming halos.
+        if (domain.row_count > 2) {
+            updateRows(domain, 1, domain.row_count - 1,
+                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
+                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
+        }
+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
+        }
+
+        updateRows(domain, 0, 1, domain.upper_halo.data(),
+                   domain.lower_halo.data(), domain.swap.data());
+                   domain.lower_halo.data(), domain.energy_swap.data(),
+                   domain.total_flux_swap.data());
+        if (domain.row_count > 1) {
+            updateRows(domain, domain.row_count - 1, domain.row_count,
+                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
+                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
+        }

+        domain.total_flux.swap(domain.total_flux_swap);

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
+    uint64_t result = 0;
+    const uint64_t global_begin =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
+        uint64_t energy_bits;
+        uint64_t flux_bits;
+        std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &domain.energy[i],
+                    sizeof(energy_bits));
+        std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &domain.total_flux[i],
+                    sizeof(flux_bits));
+        const uint64_t global_i = global_begin + i;
+        result ^= (energy_bits + global_i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
+        result ^= (flux_bits + global_i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
     }

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
+        local_energy_sum += domain.energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += domain.total_flux[i];
+        local_max = std::max(local_max, domain.energy[i]);
+        local_min = std::min(local_min, domain.energy[i]);
     }
-    
-    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
-        printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
-        return false;
+
+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
+        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid) {
+            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
     }
-    
-    printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
-    return true;
+    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    return valid != 0;

+        std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
+        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", ranks);
+        std::printf("Validation: %s\n\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
+        std::printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
+    }
+
+    Domain domain = buildSquare2D(grid_size, rank, ranks);
+    const uint64_t local_memory =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.energy.capacity() +
+                              domain.total_flux.capacity() +
+                              domain.energy_swap.capacity() +
+                              domain.total_flux_swap.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t) +
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.upper_halo.capacity() +
+                              domain.lower_halo.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t);
+    uint64_t total_memory = 0;
+    uint64_t max_rank_memory = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &total_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &max_rank_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Distributed memory usage: %.2f MB total, %.2f MB max/rank\n\n",
+                    total_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
+                    max_rank_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
+        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    runSimulation(domain, iterations);
+    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(domain);
+    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
+        const double milliseconds = seconds * 1000.0;
+        const double time_per_iteration = milliseconds / measured_iterations;
+        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
+            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * element_count) /
+                  seconds / 1e9
+            : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance:\n");
+        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
+        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second);
+        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
     }
-    
-    // Validation
-    if (validate) {
-        bool valid = validateResults(world);
-        if (!valid) {
-            return 1;
+
+    if (printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
         }
     }
-    
-    return 0;
+
+    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return valid ? 0 : 1;

codex
The implementation now uses structure-of-arrays storage so each halo row is a single contiguous MPI message and the hot loop has cache-friendly, vectorizable reads/writes. Nonblocking exchanges are overlapped with interior-row computation; the next check is compilation plus single- and multi-rank equivalence across edge cases.

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {

+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");

+        }

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

     }

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {

+        }

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
+    uint64_t result = 0;
+    const uint64_t global_begin =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;

+                    sizeof(energy_bits));

+                    sizeof(flux_bits));

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
+    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

+        local_energy_sum += domain.energy[i];
+        local_flux_sum += domain.total_flux[i];

+    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
+        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid) {
+            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
     }
-

+        std::printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
+    }
+
+    Domain domain = buildSquare2D(grid_size, rank, ranks);
+    const uint64_t local_memory =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.energy.capacity() +
+                              domain.total_flux.capacity() +
+                              domain.energy_swap.capacity() +
+                              domain.total_flux_swap.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t) +
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.upper_halo.capacity() +
+                              domain.lower_halo.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t);
+    uint64_t total_memory = 0;
+    uint64_t max_rank_memory = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &total_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &max_rank_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Distributed memory usage: %.2f MB total, %.2f MB max/rank\n\n",
+                    total_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
+                    max_rank_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
+        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
+    }
+
+    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
+    runSimulation(domain, iterations);
+    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
+    double seconds = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(domain);
+    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    if (rank == 0) {
+        const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
+        const double milliseconds = seconds * 1000.0;
+        const double time_per_iteration = milliseconds / measured_iterations;
+        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
+            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * element_count) /
+                  seconds / 1e9
+            : 0.0;
+        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
+        std::printf("Performance:\n");
+        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
+        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second);
+        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
+    }
+
+    if (printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
+        }
+    }
+
+    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return valid ? 0 : 1;
 }

+        }

+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<double> global;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
+        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
+        int offset = 0;

+            displacements[rank] = offset;

+

+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);

codex
The original arithmetic-order check caught an important detail: pre-combining `0.8 * 0.25` can alter low bits versus the reference’s two-step multiplication. I restored the exact operation sequence and confirmed the MPI result hash against an independent reference calculation. A 256×256 smoke benchmark also produced the same hash at 1, 2, and 4 ranks, with the local run dropping from about 3.20 ms to 0.69 ms at 4 ranks.

    13	
    14	using val_t = double;
    15	
    16	inline val_t computeFlux(val_t current, val_t neighbor) {
    17	    // Match the reference operation ordering (including its intermediate
    18	    // rounding) rather than pre-combining the two constants.
    19	    return (neighbor - current) * 0.8 * 1.0 * 0.25;
    20	}
    21	
    22	struct Domain {
    23	    int grid_size = 0;
    24	    int first_row = 0;
    25	    int row_count = 0;
    26	    int rank = 0;
    27	    int ranks = 1;
    28	    std::vector<val_t> energy;
    29	    std::vector<val_t> total_flux;
    30	    std::vector<val_t> energy_swap;
    31	    std::vector<val_t> total_flux_swap;
    32	    std::vector<val_t> upper_halo;
    33	    std::vector<val_t> lower_halo;
    34	};
    35	
    36	// Use a balanced, contiguous row decomposition.  Contiguous rows make both
    37	// halo messages contiguous and preserve global element ordering for output.
    38	Domain buildSquare2D(int grid_size, int rank, int ranks) {
    39	    Domain domain;
    40	    domain.grid_size = grid_size;
    41	    domain.rank = rank;
    42	    domain.ranks = ranks;
    43	
    44	    const int base = grid_size / ranks;
    45	    const int remainder = grid_size % ranks;
    46	    domain.row_count = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    47	    domain.first_row = rank * base + std::min(rank, remainder);
    48	
    49	    const size_t local_elements =
    50	        static_cast<size_t>(domain.row_count) * static_cast<size_t>(grid_size);
    51	    domain.energy.resize(local_elements);
    52	    domain.total_flux.resize(local_elements);
    53	    domain.energy_swap.resize(local_elements);
    54	    domain.total_flux_swap.resize(local_elements);
    55	    if (domain.row_count != 0) {
    56	        domain.upper_halo.resize(static_cast<size_t>(grid_size));
    57	        domain.lower_halo.resize(static_cast<size_t>(grid_size));
    58	    }
    59	    return domain;
    60	}
    61	
    62	inline void updateElement(const Domain& domain, int local_x, int y,
    63	                          const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
    64	                          val_t* energy_output, val_t* flux_output) {
    65	    const int n = domain.grid_size;
    66	    const int global_x = domain.first_row + local_x;
    67	    const size_t local_idx = static_cast<size_t>(local_x) * n + y;
    68	    const val_t current = domain.energy[local_idx];
    69	
    70	    val_t total_flux = 0.0;
    71	    const bool corner = (global_x == 0 || global_x == n - 1) &&
    72	                        (y == 0 || y == n - 1);
    73	    if (corner) {
    74	        // The two diagonal corners are inflows and the other two outflows.
    75	        total_flux = (global_x == y) ? 0.5 : -0.5;
    76	    }
    77	
    78	    // Keep the original connection order: +x, -x, +y, -y.  This preserves
    79	    // the floating-point result bit for bit, independent of the partition.
    80	    if (global_x + 1 < n) {
    81	        const val_t neighbor = (local_x + 1 < domain.row_count)
    82	            ? domain.energy[local_idx + n]
    83	            : lower[y];
    84	        total_flux += computeFlux(current, neighbor);
    85	    }
    86	    if (global_x > 0) {
    87	        const val_t neighbor = (local_x > 0)
    88	            ? domain.energy[local_idx - n]
    89	            : upper[y];
    90	        total_flux += computeFlux(current, neighbor);
    91	    }
    92	    if (y + 1 < n) {
    93	        total_flux += computeFlux(current, domain.energy[local_idx + 1]);
    94	    }
    95	    if (y > 0) {
    96	        total_flux += computeFlux(current, domain.energy[local_idx - 1]);
    97	    }
    98	
    99	    energy_output[local_idx] = current + total_flux;
   100	    flux_output[local_idx] = domain.total_flux[local_idx] + std::abs(total_flux);
   101	}
   102	
   103	void updateRows(const Domain& domain, int begin, int end,
   104	                const val_t* upper, const val_t* lower,
   105	                val_t* energy_output, val_t* flux_output) {
   106	    for (int local_x = begin; local_x < end; ++local_x) {
   107	        for (int y = 0; y < domain.grid_size; ++y) {
   108	            updateElement(domain, local_x, y, upper, lower, energy_output,
   109	                          flux_output);
   110	        }
   111	    }
   112	}
   113	
   114	void runSimulation(Domain& domain, int iterations) {
   115	    if (domain.row_count == 0) {
   116	        return;
   117	    }
   118	
   119	    const int previous = domain.first_row == 0 ? MPI_PROC_NULL : domain.rank - 1;
   120	    const int next = domain.first_row + domain.row_count == domain.grid_size
   121	        ? MPI_PROC_NULL : domain.rank + 1;
   122	    const int n = domain.grid_size;
   123	
   124	    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
   125	        MPI_Request requests[4];
   126	        int request_count = 0;
   127	
   128	        if (previous != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   129	            MPI_Irecv(domain.upper_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, previous, 101,
   130	                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   131	            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   132	                      previous, 102, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
   133	                      &requests[request_count++]);
   134	        }
   135	        if (next != MPI_PROC_NULL) {
   136	            MPI_Irecv(domain.lower_halo.data(), n, MPI_DOUBLE, next, 102,
   137	                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);
   138	            const size_t last_row = static_cast<size_t>(domain.row_count - 1) * n;
   139	            MPI_Isend(domain.energy.data() + last_row, n, MPI_DOUBLE,
   140	                      next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
   141	                      &requests[request_count++]);
   142	        }
   143	
   144	        // Rows not touching a rank boundary do not depend on incoming halos.
   145	        if (domain.row_count > 2) {
   146	            updateRows(domain, 1, domain.row_count - 1,
   147	                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
   148	                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
   149	        }
   150	        if (request_count != 0) {
   151	            MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);
   152	        }
   153	
   154	        updateRows(domain, 0, 1, domain.upper_halo.data(),
   155	                   domain.lower_halo.data(), domain.energy_swap.data(),
   156	                   domain.total_flux_swap.data());
   157	        if (domain.row_count > 1) {
   158	            updateRows(domain, domain.row_count - 1, domain.row_count,
   159	                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
   160	                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());
   161	        }
   162	        domain.energy.swap(domain.energy_swap);
   163	        domain.total_flux.swap(domain.total_flux_swap);
   164	    }
   165	}
   166	
   167	uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
   168	    uint64_t result = 0;
   169	    const uint64_t global_begin =
   170	        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;
   171	    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
   172	        uint64_t energy_bits;
   173	        uint64_t flux_bits;
   174	        std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &domain.energy[i],
   175	                    sizeof(energy_bits));
   176	        std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &domain.total_flux[i],
   177	                    sizeof(flux_bits));
   178	        const uint64_t global_i = global_begin + i;
   179	        result ^= (energy_bits + global_i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;
   180	        result ^= (flux_bits + global_i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;
   181	    }
   182	    return result;
   183	}
   184	
   185	bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
   186	    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
   187	    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;
   188	    val_t local_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();
   189	    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();
   190	    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
   191	        local_energy_sum += domain.energy[i];
   192	        local_flux_sum += domain.total_flux[i];
   193	        local_max = std::max(local_max, domain.energy[i]);
   194	        local_min = std::min(local_min, domain.energy[i]);
   195	    }
   196	
   197	    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;
   198	    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
   199	    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
   200	    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
   201	    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
   202	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   203	    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
   204	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   205	    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   206	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   207	    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
   208	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   209	
   210	    int valid = 1;
   211	    if (domain.rank == 0) {
   212	        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
   213	        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
   214	        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
   215	        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
   216	        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
   217	            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
   218	            valid = 0;
   219	        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
   220	            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
   221	        }
   222	        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
   223	            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
   224	            valid = 0;
   225	        }
   226	        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
   227	            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
   228	            valid = 0;
   229	        }
   230	        if (valid) {
   231	            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
   232	        }
   233	    }
   234	    MPI_Bcast(&valid, 1, MPI_INT, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   235	    return valid != 0;
   236	}
   237	
   238	std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
   239	    std::vector<int> counts;
   240	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   240	    std::vector<int> displacements;
   241	    std::vector<double> global;
   242	    if (domain.rank == 0) {
   243	        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
   244	        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
   245	        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
   246	        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
   247	        int offset = 0;
   248	        for (int rank = 0; rank < domain.ranks; ++rank) {
   249	            const int rows = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
   250	            counts[rank] = rows * domain.grid_size;
   251	            displacements[rank] = offset;
   252	            offset += counts[rank];
   253	        }
   254	        global.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.grid_size) * domain.grid_size);
   255	    }
   256	
   257	    MPI_Gatherv(domain.energy.data(), static_cast<int>(domain.energy.size()),
   258	                MPI_DOUBLE,
   259	                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
   260	                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   261	    return global;
   262	}
   263	
   264	void printUsage(const char* program) {
   265	    std::printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", program);
   266	    std::printf("Options:\n");
   267	    std::printf("  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\n");
   268	    std::printf("  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\n");
   269	    std::printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   270	    std::printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   271	    std::printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   272	}
   273	
   274	int main(int argc, char** argv) {
   275	    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);
   276	    int rank = 0;
   277	    int ranks = 1;
   278	    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);
   279	    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &ranks);
   280	
   281	    int grid_size = 512;
   282	    int iterations = 10;
   283	    bool validate = false;
   284	    bool printResults = false;
   285	    int parse_status = 0;
   286	    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
   287	        if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-n") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   288	            grid_size = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   289	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
   290	            iterations = std::atoi(argv[++i]);
   291	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
   292	            validate = true;
   293	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
   294	            printResults = true;
   295	        } else if (std::strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
   296	            parse_status = 2;
   297	        } else {
   298	            if (rank == 0) {
   299	                std::printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
   300	            }
   301	            parse_status = 1;
   302	        }
   303	    }
   304	    if (grid_size <= 0 || iterations < 0) {
   305	        if (rank == 0) {
   306	            std::printf("Grid size must be positive and iterations non-negative.\n");
   307	        }
   308	        parse_status = 1;
   309	    }
   310	    if (parse_status != 0) {
   311	        if (rank == 0) {
   312	            printUsage(argv[0]);
   313	        }
   314	        MPI_Finalize();
   315	        return parse_status == 2 ? 0 : 1;
   316	    }
   317	
   318	    const uint64_t element_count =
   319	        static_cast<uint64_t>(grid_size) * static_cast<uint64_t>(grid_size);
   320	    if (rank == 0) {
   321	        std::printf("Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\n");
   322	        std::printf("============================================\n");
   323	        std::printf("Grid size: %d x %d = %llu elements\n", grid_size,
   324	                    grid_size, static_cast<unsigned long long>(element_count));
   325	        std::printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
   326	        std::printf("MPI ranks: %d\n", ranks);
   327	        std::printf("Validation: %s\n\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
   328	        std::printf("Building unstructured mesh...\n");
   329	    }
   330	
   331	    Domain domain = buildSquare2D(grid_size, rank, ranks);
   332	    const uint64_t local_memory =
   333	        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.energy.capacity() +
   334	                              domain.total_flux.capacity() +
   335	                              domain.energy_swap.capacity() +
   336	                              domain.total_flux_swap.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t) +
   337	        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.upper_halo.capacity() +
   338	                              domain.lower_halo.capacity()) * sizeof(val_t);
   339	    uint64_t total_memory = 0;
   340	    uint64_t max_rank_memory = 0;
   341	    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &total_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_SUM, 0,
   342	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   343	    MPI_Reduce(&local_memory, &max_rank_memory, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_MAX, 0,
   344	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   345	    if (rank == 0) {
   346	        std::printf("Distributed memory usage: %.2f MB total, %.2f MB max/rank\n\n",
   347	                    total_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0),
   348	                    max_rank_memory / (1024.0 * 1024.0));
   349	        std::printf("Running simulation...\n");
   350	    }
   351	
   352	    MPI_Barrier(MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   353	    const double start = MPI_Wtime();
   354	    runSimulation(domain, iterations);
   355	    const double local_seconds = MPI_Wtime() - start;
   356	    double seconds = 0.0;
   357	    MPI_Reduce(&local_seconds, &seconds, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
   358	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   359	
   360	    const uint64_t local_hash = computeLocalHash(domain);
   361	    uint64_t result_hash = 0;
   362	    MPI_Reduce(&local_hash, &result_hash, 1, MPI_UINT64_T, MPI_BXOR, 0,
   363	               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
   364	
   365	    if (rank == 0) {
   366	        const int measured_iterations = std::max(iterations - 1, 1);
   367	        const double milliseconds = seconds * 1000.0;
   368	        const double time_per_iteration = milliseconds / measured_iterations;
   369	        const double giga_elements_per_second = seconds > 0.0
   370	            ? (static_cast<double>(measured_iterations) * element_count) /
   371	                  seconds / 1e9
   372	            : 0.0;
   373	        std::printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", milliseconds);
   374	        std::printf("Performance:\n");
   375	        std::printf("  Time per iteration: %.4f ms\n", time_per_iteration);
   376	        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
   377	                    giga_elements_per_second);
   378	        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
   379	                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
   380	        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
   381	                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
   382	    }
   383	
   384	    if (printResults) {
   385	        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
   386	        if (rank == 0) {
   387	            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
   388	        }
   389	    }
   390	
   391	    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
   392	    MPI_Finalize();
   393	    return valid ? 0 : 1;
   394	}

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;

+    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
+        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);

+            valid = 0;

+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid) {
+            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
     }

+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);

+                      MPI_COMM_WORLD, &requests[request_count++]);

+                      next, 101, MPI_COMM_WORLD,
+                      &requests[request_count++]);

+        if (domain.row_count > 2) {

+                       domain.upper_halo.data(), domain.lower_halo.data(),
+                       domain.energy_swap.data(), domain.total_flux_swap.data());

+        if (request_count != 0) {
+            MPI_Waitall(request_count, requests, MPI_STATUSES_IGNORE);

+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,

+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);

+            valid = 0;

+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;

codex
Implemented MPI distributed-memory parallelism while preserving the `unstructured` executable name.

Key changes:

- Balanced row-wise domain decomposition across MPI ranks.
- Nonblocking nearest-neighbor halo exchanges.
- Communication/computation overlap for interior rows.
- Structure-of-arrays storage for contiguous communication and cache efficiency.
- Distributed validation, timing, memory reporting, and result hashing.
- Ordered result gathering for `-r`.
- Exact original floating-point operation ordering.
- MPI required and linked through [CMakeLists.txt](/home/llmtest/evals/20260805-120633/unstructured_gpt-5.6-sol-medium_mpi_r3/unstructured/CMakeLists.txt:7).

Validation completed:

- Clean CMake build succeeded.
- Identical hashes at 1, 2, and 4 ranks.
- Tested more ranks than grid rows.
- Tested 1×1 grids and zero iterations.
- MPI output matched an independent reference hash.
- Local 256×256 smoke test improved from roughly 3.20 ms at one rank to 0.69 ms at four ranks.

+uint64_t computeLocalHash(const Domain& domain) {
+    uint64_t result = 0;
+    const uint64_t global_begin =
+        static_cast<uint64_t>(domain.first_row) * domain.grid_size;
+    for (size_t i = 0; i < domain.energy.size(); ++i) {
+        uint64_t energy_bits;

+        std::memcpy(&energy_bits, &domain.energy[i],
+                    sizeof(energy_bits));
+        std::memcpy(&flux_bits, &domain.total_flux[i],
+                    sizeof(flux_bits));
+        const uint64_t global_i = global_begin + i;

+bool validateResults(const Domain& domain) {
+    val_t local_energy_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t local_flux_sum = 0.0;

+    val_t local_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();

+    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_max = 0.0;
+    val_t energy_min = 0.0;
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_energy_sum, &energy_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_flux_sum, &flux_sum, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_SUM, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_max, &energy_max, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MAX, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    MPI_Reduce(&local_min, &energy_min, 1, MPI_DOUBLE, MPI_MIN, 0,
+               MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+
+    int valid = 1;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        std::printf("Validation results:\n");
+        std::printf("  Energy sum: %.12f\n", energy_sum);
+        std::printf("  Flux sum: %.2f\n", flux_sum);
+        std::printf("  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", energy_min, energy_max);
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        } else if (std::abs(energy_sum) > 1e-8) {
+            std::printf("  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\n");
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {
+            std::printf("  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\n");
+            valid = 0;
+        }
+        if (valid) {
+            std::printf("  Validation: PASSED\n");
+        }
     }

+std::vector<double> gatherEnergy(const Domain& domain) {
+    std::vector<int> counts;
+    std::vector<int> displacements;
+    std::vector<double> global;
+    if (domain.rank == 0) {
+        counts.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        displacements.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.ranks));
+        const int base = domain.grid_size / domain.ranks;
+        const int remainder = domain.grid_size % domain.ranks;
+        int offset = 0;
+        for (int rank = 0; rank < domain.ranks; ++rank) {
+            const int rows = base + (rank < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+            counts[rank] = rows * domain.grid_size;
+            displacements[rank] = offset;
+            offset += counts[rank];

+        global.resize(static_cast<size_t>(domain.grid_size) * domain.grid_size);
     }
-    return hash;
+
+    MPI_Gatherv(domain.energy.data(), static_cast<int>(domain.energy.size()),
+                MPI_DOUBLE,
+                global.data(), counts.data(), displacements.data(), MPI_DOUBLE,
+                0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);
+    return global;
 }

+        std::printf("  Elements/sec: %.4f GigaElements/s\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second);
+        std::printf("  Performance: %.4f GFLOPS\n",
+                    giga_elements_per_second * 22.0);
+        std::printf("  Result hash: %016llX\n\n",
+                    static_cast<unsigned long long>(result_hash));
+    }
+
+    if (printResults) {
+        std::vector<double> energy = gatherEnergy(domain);
+        if (rank == 0) {
+            print_results(energy, "ElementEnergy");
+        }
+    }
+
+    const bool valid = !validate || validateResults(domain);
+    MPI_Finalize();
+    return valid ? 0 : 1;
 }
